# Temporais que atingiram o Rio Grande do Sul podem chegar até São Paulo

![Temporais que atingiram o Rio Grande do Sul podem chegar até São Paulo](<https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/ebc.png?id=1709572&o=rss>)

:::

### O que é uma perna de chamada? A-Leg, B-Leg, SIP e Cobrança

![O que é uma perna de chamada? A-Leg, B-Leg, SIP e Cobrança](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=800%2Cheight=%2Cfit=scale-down%2Cgravity=auto%2Cformat=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fhns5l2f49avjtlndvnrl.png>)

Uma perna de chamada é uma conexão entre uma plataforma de voz e uma parte, como um telefone, dispositivo SIP ou operadora. Uma chamada entre duas pessoas geralmente tem duas pernas, chamadas de A-leg e B-leg, unidas por uma ponte.

### Winning an argument on a GitHub PR at 1 AM might satisfy your ego, but it costs you the next entire

day. Sleep deprivation ruins executive function, destroys emotional regulation, and makes you miss obvious edge cases. The code you write at midnight usually turns into the bug report you apologize for on Monday.

### Mercury LLM Review 2026: The Diffusion-Powered Reasoning Model Changin

![Mercury LLM Review 2026: The Diffusion-Powered Reasoning Model Changin](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fylgtib8znk7qany28qs8.png>)

g AI Most LLMs use autoregressive token generation — predicting one word at a time. Mercury takes a radically different approach: diffusion-based generation, where the model refines an entire response simultaneously.

### I built an open-source Rewardful alternative with 0% payout fees

![I built an open-source Rewardful alternative with 0% payout fees](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2F8mgi4q7mqk65fzuuypag.png>)
